# rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_2.lance

**Analyzed binary: all 500,987 bytes; line_count=null.** Byte intervals are zero-based and half-open. Pinned standard V2_2 binary fixture: 4,097 rows, five physical columns,25 pages (23 mini-block pages and two constants),71 mini-blocks. Every scalar, packed bit, level/index, offset, dictionary, blob description/payload, padding, schema/metadata/table/footer byte is interpreted. No callable symbols.

[Module contract](../../../../../L1-modules/archive/rust-lance-file-standard-v22-fixture.md) · [Format specification](../../../../../L3-symbols/format-spec.md) · [Behavior contracts](../../../../../L3-symbols/behavior-contracts.md) · [Cookbook](../../../../../L4-crosscutting/cookbook.md) · [Coverage ledger](../../../../../archive/pre-v21/MANIFEST.json)

SHA-256: `8298cd9301e657417b0725461345c27cf46515529d2a8b35824be139e3466a14`. Offline KB-side artifact interpretation only. No source build, test, writer, reader or generator executed. All six lance-file fixture binaries are now interpreted, but complete encoding/IO implementations and Dataset integration remain pending. Baseline pin/hash does not independently qualify released-package provenance.

[Preserved bytes](../../../../../evidence/binary/v2_2.lance), [decoded rows](../../../../../evidence/binary/v2_2-decoded-rows.json), [all page/chunk/level/schema/wire details](../../../../../analysis/iteration-070-v2_2-fixture.json), [semantic decoder](../../../../../tools/analyze-standard-v22-fixture.mjs), [independent assembler](../../../../../tools/verify-standard-v22-reconstruction.mjs).

## Complete logical contract

Rows r=0..4096: id=r; name=null for r%7=0 else value-NNNN-deterministic-fixture (32 ASCII bytes); items=null for r%11=0 else [r,(null if r%5=0 else2*r),3*r]; category=null for r%13=0 else [red,green,blue][r%3]; blob always blob-NNNN-deterministic-payload (31 ASCII bytes). NNNN is r padded to four decimal digits. Counts:586 null names,373 null lists,11172 physical children with745 nulls,316 null categories; no empty lists or null/empty blobs. Lists have11545 level entries because each null list adds one invisible marker. (rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/datagen.rs:37-99; rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_2.lance@bytes:0-500987)

Each of five batches (1024,1024,1024,1024,1 rows) writes blob payloads first, then one page for id,name,items,category,blob. Every blob uses64 bytes:31 payload plus33 bytes0x48; batch bases0,123648,247936,372608,497408. Page buffers independently align to64 with0x48, excluded from stored sizes. Mini-block components align to8 with0xFE. The data region[0,498048) consists of233644 page-buffer bytes (including chunk framing/internal padding),2196 outer page-padding bytes,127007 blob bytes and135201 blob-padding bytes. (rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/datagen.rs:102-131; rust/lance-encoding/src/encoder.rs:125-165; rust/lance-encoding/src/encodings/logical/primitive.rs:5751-5910; rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_2.lance@bytes:0-498048)

The final id page stores inline Int32 4096 with no buffers; final name page stores a52-byte nonnull Utf8 scalar buffer. Their ConstantLayout layers are AllValidItem. All other pages use MiniBlock (inside BlobLayout for blob) with has_large_chunk=true: UInt32 metadata words and value sizes, but UInt16 level count and rep/def sizes. Six flattened schema field identities map to five physical columns; items and child item share column2. Footer(2,2), A498375,B500851,C500931,globals1,columns5,LANC. (protos/encodings_v2_1.proto:104-161; protos/encodings_v2_1.proto:288-358; rust/lance-arrow/src/scalar.rs:61-136; rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_2.lance@bytes:498048-500987)

## Complete mini-block catalog

UInt32 metadata low4 bits hold log(values), with0 meaning the remaining physical slots. High28 bits store chunk_bytes/8-1, allowing up to2^31 chunk bytes. Full final chunks may keep a positive log; full blob chunks all use8. Each header has UInt16 num_levels, optional UInt16 rep_size and def_size, then one UInt32 value_buffer_size. Each component pads separately to8 bytes. Stored value slots and level entries differ for nullable lists. The two constant pages have no mini-block metadata/header. ## Every byte region

### Bytes 0-65536

Batch0 rows[0,1024): 1024 records of64 bytes; each contains31 ASCII bytes blob-NNNN-deterministic-payload followed by33 bytes0x48. NNNN is the global row number padded to4 decimal digits. No prefix or compression; descriptions store position=0+64*local_row,size31.

Evidence: rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_2.lance@bytes:0-65536. Interpretation: rust/lance-encoding/src/encoder.rs:125-165; rust/lance-encoding/src/encodings/logical/blob.rs:105-203. SHA-256: `1b7e127820f99e28dd148886da54489473de3237dcd392079c431a5e8c258217`.

### Bytes 65536-65540

Column0 page0 chunk0: little-endian UInt32 2576; low4 bits0 are the final-chunk sentinel; remaining page values=1024; high28 bits encode1296/8-1=161. This indexes chunk[65600,66896).

Evidence: rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_2.lance@bytes:65536-65540. Interpretation: rust/lance-encoding/src/encodings/logical/primitive.rs:5751-5910. SHA-256: `4bb3cc6cdd899719ff0390e0b5cdd344c5cd6582a809e32da82b0d5f0acca43b`.

### Bytes 65608-66892

Column0 page0 chunk0. 1024 32-bit stored values, encodingInlineBitpacking; blocks [{"start":65608,"end":66892,"kind":"inline","bits":32,"width":10,"count":1024,"zero_tail_values":0}]. Inline blocks store their bit width as a native-width little-endian header followed by128*width FastLanes bytes. Padded1024-value tails decode to zero. Null child/key backing values are0; logical validity comes from definition levels.
